# Source reads, October 2026

The ledger the plan's decision D12 asks for: agents never bump `verifiedAt`; every fact an agent checks or corrects is recorded here
with the URL, the read date and a short quote from the page, so an editor can confirm it before re-verifying the item.
Each lane appends its own section; keep earlier sections unchanged.

## CNT-EVENTS · official-page reads, 2026-10-09 (PT)

Scope: every event happening 10/16–11/15 (235), every dated offer in that window (58) and every ongoing offer (100). Each official
page was read with one read-only GET on 2026-10-09 (PT, ~14:05–14:40) by the CNT-EVENTS lane agent; no forms, logins or posts.
"Monitor" is the status `GET /api/sources/freshness` reported the same afternoon. Quotes are short excerpts of the page text.
Outcome counts: match 269 · not machine-readable 73 · date not located in page text 28 · reviewed 10 · corrected 9 · unreachable 3 · no change 1.
Corrections are applied in `src/data/editorial-overlay` (`fix`) and listed in the lane PR; `verifiedAt` is never changed (D12).
The Presidio time correction is also applied where the overlay cannot reach: the planner schedule in
`src/data/november-refresh-east-sf-planning.json` and the city bulletin in `src/data/city-current-east-sf.json` (+ its English).
The calendar schedule follows the overlay's `fix.occurrenceDates` (the postponed South San Francisco State of the City).
Pages that could not be read by script (HTTP 403 bot walls, script-rendered calendars) keep their last human-verified facts and need a
human re-read if the item falls within the next 3 days.

## CNT-SUPPLY · official-page reads, 2026-10-12 (PT)

Read by the CNT-SUPPLY lane agent with read-only GETs on 2026-10-12 (PT, ~00:45–02:00); no forms, logins, sign-ups or posts.
"Monitor" is the row status `GET /api/sources/freshness` returned at ~00:50 (`!` = needsReview). `verifiedAt` of existing items is unchanged (D12);
the new items carry `verifiedAt` 2026-10-12 because their official pages were read that day. Library event facts come from the library's
own Bibliocommons record of that event page (the public `gateway.bibliocommons.com/v2/libraries/<library>/events` feed the page renders),
and branch addresses and map pins from the same library's location records (`…/locations`). Two city map fallbacks were added from the
Census 2026 places gazetteer (Bolinas CDP 0607316: 37.917725, -122.709502; Castro Valley CDP 0611964: 37.708271, -122.062751;
Crockett CDP 0617274: 38.051919, -122.220127), stored rounded to three decimals in src/data/calendar-city-locations.ts.

Review round 1 (2026-10-12, ~12:15–14:30 PT, same read-only GETs): the San Jose Veterans Day parade page (route and FAQ start time,
now in the item), a fresh read of every new item that says a fee, time or route is not published (the claims hold: library feed
descriptions carry no fee; Petaluma and Belmont list no hours), the Old Post Office /about and /menus pages, and the decisive line
for each re-check row below (the first pass had quoted page navigation). Three offers changed on re-reading: Poppy & Claro,
Cal Performances Golden Bear Circle and LEGO Hillsdale (rows marked corrected).

Review round 2 (2026-10-12, ~16:05 PT, same read-only GETs): the sfpl.org/events listing, whose policy line is the basis for "免费" on
the eight SFPL items (policy row after the new-items table; the SFPL event pages themselves state no fee, and their "Attending Programs"
line "All programs are drop-in (no registration necessary) unless otherwise noted" is the basis for "不用报名"); the North Beach open
house page, whose ghost walking tour says "Registration Required." (item corrected: the tour needs registration, the rest is drop-in);
and Poppy & Claro (page unchanged; the offer now ends 10/30 at the latest, see its row). The Apple workshop and the three Westfield
Valley Fair rows now quote their decisive lines.

## CNT-SUPPLY-2 · official-page reads, 2026-10-13 (PT)

Read by the CNT-SUPPLY-2 lane agent with read-only GETs on 2026-10-13 (PT); no forms, logins, registrations or posts. The new items carry
`verifiedAt` 2026-10-13 because their official or organiser pages were read that day; no existing item's `verifiedAt` changed. Library
event facts come from each library's own Bibliocommons record of the event (the public `gateway.bibliocommons.com/v2/libraries/<library>/events`
feed the event page renders, pulled 20:55–20:59 PT), and the event pages themselves were fetched at 21:15 PT (HTTP column). Branch
addresses and map pins come from the same library's location records (`…/locations`, read 21:09 PT); the FOPAL sale's pin is the Palo Alto
feed's own place record for Cubberley Community Center (4000 Middlefield Road, 37.4184094, -122.1084361). Sonoma County Library items
come from its LibraryCalendar event pages (21:03 PT); those pages give an address but no coordinates, so these items have no pin and
the calendar map places them at their city. Two city reference points were added from the Census 2026 places gazetteer (GET 21:39 PT,
https://www2.census.gov/geo/docs/maps-data/data/gazetteer/2026_Gazetteer/2026_gaz_place_06.txt): Forestville CDP 0624960 (38.482491,
-122.889884) and Guerneville CDP 0631470 (38.517165, -122.989897), plus Gilroy city 0629504 (37.006407, -121.585321) and Saratoga
city 0670280 (37.268328, -122.026223), whose library events have venue pins but whose towns had no city reference yet; stored
rounded to three decimals in src/data/calendar-city-locations.ts.
SFPL event pages were read 21:00–21:10 PT; the organiser pages (Dickens Fair, Calistoga, Pleasanton) 21:07–21:08 PT. "feed" in the
HTTP column means a further date of the same programme whose record was read in the feed, not fetched as a page.

San Francisco, 11/21–11/28 (the last three rows; `verifiedAt` 2026-10-14): SF events dated on or before 11/30 count in the /opus-bay
world check (W6-S1, `tests/opus-bay-w6-s-venues.test.ts`), which needs the Opus session's placement decision for each one, so these
three ship with their ids in `WORLD_IMPORT_PENDING` (`src/data/public-catalog-holdout.ts`): the site lists them, /opus-bay's file does
not. Their pages were read on 2026-10-13 (21:00–21:10 PT) and re-read on 2026-10-14 at 11:48 PT (all four 200, same dates, times,
rooms and drop-in line, no cancellation); the SFPL events listing's free line was re-read at 11:51 PT. The other SF items are December ones.

Earlier weekends (10/24–11/15), re-checked the same evening: the 36 live items whose source is a Bibliocommons event are all still in
their library's feed and none is marked cancelled; the nine SFPL pages and two Sonoma County Library pages (GET 21:32 PT) show no
cancellation (the Marina qigong page keeps its standing rain note).

East Bay farmers' markets, read 2026-10-14 04:05–04:10 PT (the three market rows; `verifiedAt` 2026-10-14): the market operators' own
pages (Ecology Center for Berkeley, Agricultural Institute of Marin for Grand Lake, Pacific Coast Farmers' Market Association for
Irvington). Each page gives the weekday and hours of a weekly market and lists no holiday closure, so the items list the nine market
days from 10/17–18 to 12/12–13. Map pins are the operators' own markers: the Ecology Center page's map widget (Downtown Berkeley,
37.869692, -122.272792), AIM's Google Maps link for Splash Pad Park (37.8105444, -122.2478409) and PCFMA's static-map marker for
4039 Bay St. (37.5327762, -121.9600102). None of the three pages states an entry fee, so cost stays "unknown".

H69 alignment, 2026-10-14 (PT): CNT-HOTFIX-2 (#41) marked library programmes free where the library system publishes that policy
(its "H69 · library programme policies" rows). The 19 batch items in those systems whose 10-13 label said only 活动页没有写费用 /
"No fee is listed" now follow the same rule: cost "free", label 图书馆活动免费 / "Free library program" (sales: 免费入场 / "Free entry"
with the goods priced), planner admission $0; `verifiedAt` stays 2026-10-13. Before the change the agent re-read the five policy pages
(19:24 PT, every quote above present), the ten Sonoma County Library event pages (19:24 PT: dates present, no fee or cancellation
wording) and the SMCL, SJPL, SCCLD and Marin Bibliocommons feeds (19:25 PT: the nine records present, none cancelled, every listed
date still there, no fee wording). Items of other library systems keep "unknown" unless their own page names a price: #41 found no
such policy for Alameda County or Contra Costa, and Oakland's and Palo Alto's policies were not part of the H69 read.

Edition: `MONTHLY_EDITION.throughDate` 2026-11-30 → 2026-12-31 and label 2026 年秋冬 · 收录至 12/31 (`src/data/monthly-settings.ts`), so the
December items are listed; `updatedAt` 2026-10-14 is the day of this batch's latest reads (the markets). `checkedAt` stays 2026-10-07.

## CNT-GUIDES-2 · official-page reads for the guide refresh and three new elder guides, 2026-10-14 (PT)

Scope: the 13 guides last dated 2026-05-22 or 2026-06-10 (H13), the BART October bulletin, and the three new 长辈与家属 guides (H71):
elder scam prevention, senior transit discounts and the Chinese-language resource map. Each page was read on 2026-10-14 between about
21:20 and 22:10 PT by the CNT-GUIDES-2 lane agent with headless Chrome or a read-only GET; the FTC pages answer 403 to headless Chrome, so
they were read with a fetch tool and the quote is the sentence it returned. No forms, logins or posts. Guides carry `updatedAt` (content
date), not `verifiedAt`; D12 is unaffected. Numbers and phones are also registered in `src/data/guide-editorial/official-figures.ts`
(test: tests/guide-pillars.test.ts) and listed for the owner in the lane PR under 新官方数字（请店主过目）. The 30-second records
added for H08 repeat only facts already printed in each article (tests/guide-editorial.test.ts). Second pass: lanes/CNT-GUIDES-2/crosscheck.md.

### CNT-GUIDES-2 second-pass clauses (review 2026-10-15)

The second pass (lanes/CNT-GUIDES-2/crosscheck.md §3) confirmed the clauses below, but the table above had no quote for them, so a
later audit could not trace them from this file. The quotes come from the lane's saved headless-Chrome reads of the same pages,
2026-10-14 between about 21:17 and 21:41 PT. The FTC page answers 403 to headless Chrome; its quote is from a fetch-tool read on
2026-10-15. One clause had no official sentence behind it: the IC3 line "钱没被骗走也可以报" now says what IC3 writes ("file a report
even if you are unsure of whether your complaint qualifies"), in zh and en. No other fact changed.

## CNT-SUPPLY-3 · official-page reads, 2026-10-15 (PT)

Batch `src/data/supply-2026-10-15-*` (12/14/2026–1/3/2027 and the 11/21–12/13 gaps) and dated updates of 15 existing series. The
lead list was GPT's research handoff (`opus-qa/gpt/out-1015`, `holiday-supply.csv` 267 rows and `additional-chinese-events.csv`),
used as untrusted evidence only: every item below was re-read on 2026-10-15 from its organiser's or library's own page or public
event record, except the facts marked "read by GPT editor review 2026-10-15" (those quote GPT's own reading of an official page or
calendar that this lane's plain GET could not render, and say which page). Reads, all read-only GETs from this PC:
- Bibliocommons public events feeds (`gateway.bibliocommons.com/v2/libraries/<lib>/events`) for Oakland, Contra Costa, Alameda
  County, San José, Santa Clara County, San Mateo County, Palo Alto, Marin and San Mateo Public Library, 17:44 PT, window
  11/21–1/3, with each library's special hours (closures). No session is listed on a day the library's own special hours mark
  closed: SJPL 11/26–27, 12/24–25, 1/1; SCCLD 11/26–27, 12/24–25, 12/31; SMCL 11/26–27, 12/25–27, 1/1; Oakland 11/26–27, 12/24–25,
  12/31, 1/1; Marin 11/26–27, 12/24–25, 12/31, 1/1; Palo Alto Children's/College Terrace/Downtown 12/24–1/1. Where a feed lists a
  record inside a closure (Oakland toddler storytime 12/24, 12/31; Calabazas Mandarin storytime 11/26, 12/24; San Carlos storytime
  12/27; the Millbrae study group 12/26), the record is left out.
- SFPL day listings for 12/14–1/3 and their event pages (17:4x–18:1x PT); 1/1–1/3 list "No events found." yet.
- Sonoma County Library day listings for the six weekend days and event pages (18:0x PT).
- Organiser pages (17:3x–18:4x PT; HTTP status in the table). marincounty.gov and gracecathedral.org answered 403 to a plain fetch
  and 200 to a browser-header GET.
- Programme-fee policy pages (18:2x PT), the basis of "图书馆活动免费 / Free library program" (#41's H69 rule; Oakland's policy as
  W6-INT's ledger rows record it):

source file and `verifiedAt` are unchanged; the overlay entry gets the added sessions and a dated `fix` (occurrenceDates, endDate,
dateLabel, and a plan line where the old line counted the old run), and the batch's planning file extends the planner schedule.

(its description says Thursdays, its records are Saturdays); Alameda County "Music Hour" 12/26 (description says second Saturdays);

Canyon Train of Lights (sold out for the season), Walnut Creek on Ice (old and new season pages disagree), SF Symphony holiday
programmes, Six Flags WinterFest 1/2–3 (no hours published), and adult-only or teen-only feed rows.

overnight camp (Camp Newman allows no drop-in or day use; per-person per-camp-day rates with lodging and meals; never listed as free
or as a day trip); the Belmont Mandarin bilingual storytime gains its 10/21, 11/4 and 11/18 sessions; the Millbrae Mandarin/Cantonese
storytime is already on main with the same two October dates. Neither storytime meets on the 1/2–3 weekend.

## GPT editor review 2026-10-15 · weekend source review (10/17–18, 10/24–25)

The weekly Wednesday source review that `ROADMAP-8-9.md` §2.5 assigns to the owner or the GPT editor. The GPT editor read the
official page of every live item on 10/17–18 and 10/24–25 (135 ids) on 2026-10-15 and compared it with the BAYLINK detail page
(`opus-qa/gpt/out-1015/weekend-review.csv`). Its outcome: 115 consistent, 6 not consistent, 1 sold out, 1 cancelled, 12 unreadable.
"Consistent" covers the fields the reviewer could read on the page (date, time, place, price or registration where the page
states them); fields the page does not state stay as they were. This section records the rows marked consistent: these items
now carry `verifiedAt` 2026-10-15 (113 items). Reader: GPT editor review 2026-10-15 (not re-read by the lane agent).
Not included: `sf-doja-cat-2026` and `sf-thrive-football-sunday-2026` (the reviewer saw only the search index of the live
pages, so their `verifiedAt` stays), the 7 corrected items (section "CNT-WEEKEND-1015 · weekend corrections" above),
Thrive City Thrill-O-Ween (owner decision pending) and the 12 unreadable items (HTTP 403 or an empty page shell; unchanged).
Quotes are the reviewer's short excerpts; " / " joins excerpts from different places on the same page.
Schedule dates (`planning.schedule.verifiedAt`) are not edited by hand: the six items whose source module derives the schedule's
date from the item's own `verifiedAt` (Daly City, Palo Alto Pet Palooza, SFPL OMI, FOG Diwali, Napa Tulocay, SFPL Western
Addition) follow it to 2026-10-15; schedules that carry their own read date keep it.

The third weekly Wednesday source review (`ROADMAP-8-9.md` §2.5). The GPT editor read the official page of every live item on
11/7–8, 11/14–15 and 11/21–22 (130 ids, 223 dated occurrences) on 2026-10-16 PT and compared it with the BAYLINK detail page
(`opus-qa/gpt/out-1016d/weekend-review-1107.csv`). Its outcome: 84 consistent (可核), 35 partly read (部分核实), 7 to update
(需更新) and 4 official-source conflicts (官网冲突). "Consistent" means the reviewer confirmed the date, time, price, place and
status fields the site states for the target days, with an official URL and a short quote; fields the page does not state stay
as they were. This section records those 84 rows: the items now carry `verifiedAt` 2026-10-16. Reader: GPT editor review
2026-10-16 (not re-read by the lane agent, except Red Oak Victory, re-read in the section above with the same result).

7 corrected items and the 4 conflicts (section "CNT-WEEKEND-1107 · November weekend corrections" above). Evidence types are the
reviewer's (A = official page text, A1 = the official event page or the library's public calendar entry for that day). Quotes
are the reviewer's short excerpts; ";" joins excerpts from the same page. Schedule dates (`planning.schedule.verifiedAt`) are
not edited by hand.
